back button returns to Operations Center (preserving scroll)\n```\n\n**Pattern D — Cascading Filter (Analytics → Domain)**\n\n```\nAnalytics: \"Dispute rate spike +40% this week\"\n  → click metric\n  → navigates to /admin/bookings?filter=disputed&date=this-week\n  → Booking Center loads with filter pre-applied\n  → notification: \"Filter applied from Analytics. Clear filter →\"\n```\n\n**Pattern E — Cross-Entity Action**\n\n```\nModeration Center: resolve complaint → trigger refund\n  → \"Yêu cầu hoàn tiền 100%\" button\n  → modal confirmation\n  → confirmed → Finance Center API called\n  → toast: \"Đã tạo lệnh hoàn tiền RF-0042. Xem trong Finance Center →\"\n  → user stays in Moderation Center\n  → Finance Center processes in background\n```\n\n### 3.3 Sub-Navigation per Domain\n\n```\nTutor Center:\n  /admin/tutors                   ← All Tutors (default)\n  /admin/tutors/pending           ← Pending Approval\n  /admin/tutors/suspended         ← Suspended & Rejected\n\nService Center:\n  /admin/services                 ← All Services\n  /admin/services/courses         ← Recorded Courses\n  /admin/services/group-classes   ← Group Classes\n  /admin/services/one-on-one      ← 1-on-1 Listings\n  /admin/services/documents       ← Documents\n  /admin/services/subjects        ← Subject Catalog\n\nBooking Center:\n  /admin/bookings                 ← All Bookings\n  /admin/bookings/live            ← Live Today\n  /admin/bookings/calendar        ← Calendar View\n  /admin/bookings/disputes        ← Disputes\n\nFinance Center:\n  /admin/finance                  ← Overview\n  /admin/finance/transactions     ← All Transactions\n  /admin/finance/payouts          ← Tutor Payouts\n  /admin/finance/refunds          ← Refunds\n  /admin/finance/promotions       ← Commissions & Promotions\n  /admin/finance/fraud            ← Fraud & Reconciliation\n\nModeration Center:\n  /admin/moderation               ← Overview (AI Operations Dashboard)\n  /admin/moderation/complaints    ← Complaints\n  /admin/moderation/reviews       ← Reported Reviews\n  /admin/moderation/violations    ← Content Violations\n  /admin/moderation/appeals       ← Appeals\n\nAnalytics:\n  /admin/analytics/platform       ← Platform Health\n  /admin/analytics/users          ← User Analytics\n  /admin/analytics/tutors         ← Tutor Analytics\n  /admin/analytics/matching       ← Matching Analytics\n  /admin/analytics/finance        ← Revenue Analytics\n  /admin/analytics/ai             ← AI Performance\n  /admin/analytics/reports        ← Scheduled Reports\n\nSettings:\n  /admin/settings/general         ← General\n  /admin/settings/subjects        ← Subject Catalog\n  /admin/settings/commission      ← Commission Rules\n  /admin/settings/gateways        ← Payment Gateways\n  /admin/settings/email-templates ← Email Templates\n  /admin/settings/accounts        ← Admin Accounts & RBAC\n  /admin/settings/feature-flags   ← Feature Flags\n  /admin/settings/ai-config       ← AI Configuration\n  /admin/settings/audit-log       ← System Audit Log\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 4 — ENTITY RELATIONSHIPS\n\n### 4.1 Core Entity Map\n\n```\nusers ──────────────────────── 1 role (student/parent/tutor/admin)\n  │\n  ├── (tutor) ──► tutor_profiles ──► service_listings\n  │                                        │\n  │                                        ▼\n  │                                   [5 service types]\n  │                                   recorded_course\n  │                                   group_class\n  │                                   one_on_one\n  │                                   offline_class\n  │                                   document\n  │\n  ├── (student) ──► tutor_requests ──────────────────────┐\n  │                                                       │ AI matching\n  │                                                       ▼\n  │                                               matched_tutors[]\n  │\n  ├── (student/parent) ──► bookings ◄──────────────────────────────┐\n  │                            │                                    │\n  │                            ▼                                    │\n  │                       transactions                              │\n  │                            │                                    │\n  │                            ├── wallet (student debit)          │\n  │                            ├── wallet (tutor credit)           │\n  │                            └── escrow (held during session)    │\n  │                                                                 │\n  │                       disputes ◄── booking.status=DISPUTED ────┘\n  │                            │\n  │                            ▼\n  │                       refunds (partial/full)\n  │\n  └── (any) ──► complaints ──► [linked to booking/service/tutor]\n                    │\n                    ▼\n              ai_assessments\n                    │\n                    ▼\n              audit_logs\n```\n\n### 4.2 Entity Attribute Summary\n\n```\ntutor_requests:\n  id, student_id, subject, education_level, grade,\n  learning_format, city, budget_min, budget_max,\n  schedule_preferences (JSON), learning_goals (JSON),\n  created_at, converted_booking_id (NULL until booked)\n\nbookings:\n  id, student_id, tutor_id, service_id,\n  session_date, duration_minutes,\n  format (online/offline), location,\n  status (requested|confirmed|in_progress|completed|cancelled|disputed),\n  amount, escrow_status (held|released|refunded),\n  cancellation_reason, created_at, updated_at\n\ntransactions:\n  id, booking_id, type (payment|refund|payout|commission),\n  amount, currency (VND),\n  gateway (vnpay|wallet|bank_transfer),\n  gateway_txn_id, status (initiated|processing|completed|failed),\n  created_at\n\nwithdrawals:\n  id, tutor_id, amount, bank_account (JSON),\n  status (requested|pending_verification|approved|processing|completed|rejected),\n  rejected_reason, processed_at\n\ncomplaints:\n  id, case_id (human-readable: CASE-2026-XXX),\n  reporter_id, reporter_role (student|tutor|parent),\n  target_id, target_type (tutor|student|service|system),\n  title, content, attachments[],\n  severity (CRITICAL|HIGH|MEDIUM|LOW),\n  status (OPEN|AI_ASSESSED|AWAITING_ADMIN|IN_REVIEW|RESOLVED|CLOSED_BY_AI|REOPENED),\n  ai_result (JSON), linked_dispute_id, linked_booking_id,\n  assigned_to (admin_id), created_at, resolved_at, sla_deadline\n```\n\n---\n\n## SECTION 5 — ENTITY LIFECYCLES\n\n### 5.1 Tutor Lifecycle\n\n```\n                    [REGISTERED]\n                         │ submits tutor-profile form\n                         ▼\n                   [PENDING_REVIEW]\n                    ┌────┴────┐\n                    ▼         ▼\n              [APPROVED]   [REJECTED]\n                    │              │ reapply (after 30 days)\n                    │              └──────────────────┐\n                    ▼                                 ▼\n              [ACTIVE] ◄──────────────────── [PENDING_REVIEW]\n                    │\n             ┌──────┼──────┐\n             ▼      ▼      ▼\n        [FLAGGED] [WARN_1] [WARN_2]\n             │      │        │\n             │      └────────┘\n             ▼           │\n       [SUSPENDED]  (3rd violation)\n             │                  │\n    appealed │   appeal rejected│\n             ▼                  ▼\n      [APPEAL_REVIEW]     [OFFBOARDED]\n             │\n    ┌────────┴────────┐\n    ▼                 ▼\n[REINSTATED]    [OFFBOARDED]\n\nTriggers:\n  PENDING_REVIEW → APPROVED:   Admin action + email sent\n  PENDING_REVIEW → REJECTED:   Admin action + reason + email sent\n  ACTIVE → FLAGGED:            Complaint threshold exceeded (auto)\n  ACTIVE → WARN_1:             1st violation confirmed\n  ACTIVE → WARN_2:             2nd violation confirmed\n  ACTIVE → SUSPENDED:          3rd violation OR critical violation\n  SUSPENDED → OFFBOARDED:      No appeal within 30 days\n  Any status → OFFBOARDED:     Super_admin manual action\n```\n\n### 5.2 Service Lifecycle\n\n```\n              [DRAFT]\n                 │ tutor publishes\n                 ▼\n             [ACTIVE]\n          ┌──────┼──────┐\n          ▼      ▼      ▼\n     [FLAGGED] [HIDDEN] [ARCHIVED]\n          │      │\n          ▼      │\n  [UNDER_REVIEW] │\n     ┌────┴───┐  │\n     ▼        ▼  ▼\n[REINSTATED] [REMOVED]\n\nNotes:\n  DRAFT → ACTIVE:       Tutor action (self-publish after approval)\n  ACTIVE → FLAGGED:     User report OR AI content check\n  ACTIVE → HIDDEN:      Admin action (temporary hide)\n  ACTIVE → ARCHIVED:    Tutor action OR admin action\n  FLAGGED → UNDER_REVIEW: Admin opens review\n  UNDER_REVIEW → REINSTATED: Admin clears\n  UNDER_REVIEW → REMOVED: Admin confirms violation\n```\n\n### 5.3 Course Lifecycle (subtype of Service)\n\n```\n           [DRAFT]\n              │ tutor publishes\n              ▼\n          [PUBLISHED]\n        ┌──────┼──────┐\n        ▼      ▼      ▼\n  [FLAGGED] [HIDDEN] [ARCHIVED]\n        │\n        ▼\n  [UNDER_CONTENT_REVIEW]\n        ┌────┴───┐\n        ▼        ▼\n[REINSTATED]  [REMOVED]\n                   │\n                   ▼\n            [REFUND_INITIATED]  ← trigger: refund all enrollees\n\nNotes:\n  Course is a Service with additional tracking:\n    → enrollment_count, completion_rate, rating\n  REMOVED triggers refund_policy check:\n    → if enrolled students exist → REFUND_INITIATED → Finance Center\n```\n\n### 5.4 Booking Lifecycle\n\n```\n           [REQUESTED]\n          ┌────┴────┐\n          ▼          ▼\n    [CONFIRMED]  [REJECTED_BY_TUTOR]\n          │         (escrow released)\n    ┌─────┤\n    ▼     ▼\n[CANCELLED] [IN_PROGRESS]\n(policy      │\n applies)    ▼\n         [COMPLETED]\n          ┌────┴────┐\n          ▼          ▼\n  [REVIEWED]    [DISPUTED]\n  (normal end)        │\n                ┌─────┴─────┐\n                ▼           ▼\n      [RESOLVED_REFUND] [RESOLVED_RELEASE]\n         (escrow→student)  (escrow→tutor)\n                       ▼\n                   (PARTIAL)  ← split escrow\n\nCancellation timing policy:\n  >24h before session: full refund\n  2-24h before session: 50% refund\n  <2h before session: no refund (tutor protected)\n  Tutor cancels anytime: full refund + compensation\n```\n\n### 5.5 Transaction Lifecycle\n\n```\n       [INITIATED]\n            │ gateway call\n            ▼\n       [PROCESSING]\n        ┌────┴────┐\n        ▼          ▼\n  [COMPLETED]   [FAILED]\n        │              │ retry logic\n        │              └──► [INITIATED] (max 3 retries)\n        ▼\n  [SETTLED]  ← escrow released\n        │\n        └──► [REFUND_INITIATED] ← dispute resolution\n                    │\n                    ▼\n             [REFUND_PROCESSING]\n                    │\n                    ▼\n             [REFUNDED]  ← terminal state\n\nNotes:\n  FAILED after 3 retries → notify Finance Center → fraud check\n  COMPLETED → escrow status: HELD until session completion\n  SETTLED → escrow status: RELEASED\n```\n\n### 5.6 Refund Lifecycle\n\n```\n         [REQUESTED]\n         (by user or triggered by admin)\n              │\n              ▼\n      [PENDING_FINANCE_REVIEW]\n          ┌────┴────┐\n          ▼          ▼\n      [APPROVED]   [REJECTED]\n          │              │ notification to requester\n          ▼\n      [PROCESSING]\n      (gateway/wallet call)\n          │\n          ▼\n      [COMPLETED]  ← money returned to source\n\nTriggers:\n  Dispute resolved in student's favor → auto REQUESTED\n  Admin action in Moderation → REQUESTED\n  Finance manager review → APPROVED / REJECTED\n```\n\n### 5.7 Complaint Lifecycle\n\n```\n           [SUBMITTED]\n                │\n                ▼\n          [AI_EVALUATING]\n          (buildCaseContext → evaluateCase)\n                │\n        ┌───────┴───────┐\n        ▼               ▼\n[CLOSED_BY_AI]    [AWAITING_ADMIN]\n(autoResolved=true)       │\n        │           ┌─────┴─────┐\n        │           ▼           ▼\n        │      [IN_REVIEW]  [ESCALATED]\n        │           │           │\n        │     ┌─────┴─────┐     │\n        │     ▼           ▼     ▼\n        │ [RESOLVED]  [DISPUTED] (→ Booking Center)\n        │\n        └──────────► [REOPENED] ← user disagrees with resolution\n                          │\n                          ▼\n                     [IN_REVIEW]  (re-enters queue)\n\nSLA timers (from AI_CONFIG):\n  CRITICAL: 1 hour\n  HIGH:
